1	import type { TaskInstanceState } from '../core';
2	import { transition } from '../core';
3	import type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
4	import type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
5	
6	// --- Lifecycle context ---
7	
8	export interface LifecycleContext {
9	  state: TaskStateContainer;
10	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
11	  abortResolvers: Map<string, () => void>;
12	  settleResolvers: Map<string, () => void>;
13	  now: () => string;
14	}
15	
16	export type UpdateLifecycleFn = (
17	  instanceId: string,
18	  action: 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail',
19	  extra?: Partial<TaskInstanceState>,
20	) => TaskInstanceState | undefined;
21	
22	export function createLifecycleManager(ctx: LifecycleContext) {
23	  // --- Abort / settle ---
24	
25	  function createAbortSignal(instanceId: string): Promise<void> {
26	    return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
27	      ctx.abortResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
28	    });
29	  }
30	
31	  function abortInstance(instanceId: string): void {
32	    const resolve = ctx.abortResolvers.get(instanceId);
33	    if (resolve) {
34	      ctx.abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
35	      resolve();
36	    }
37	  }
38	
39	  function resolveSettle(instanceId: string): void {
40	    const resolve = ctx.settleResolvers.get(instanceId);
41	    if (resolve) {
42	      ctx.settleResolvers.delete(instanceId);
43	      resolve();
44	    }
45	  }
46	
47	  // --- Lifecycle helpers ---
48	
49	  const updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn = (
50	    instanceId,
51	    action,
52	    extra?,
53	  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined => {
54	    const instance = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
55	    if (!instance) return undefined;
56	    const newStatus = transition(instance.lifecycle, action);
57	    const timestampField =
58	      action === 'start' ? 'startedAt' :
59	      action === 'pause' ? 'pausedAt' :
60	      action === 'stop' ? 'stoppedAt' :
61	      action === 'complete' ? 'completedAt' :
62	      action === 'fail' ? 'failedAt' : undefined;
63	    return ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, {
64	      lifecycle: newStatus,
65	      ...(timestampField ? { [timestampField]: ctx.now() } : {}),
66	      ...extra,
67	    });
68	  };
69	
70	  function completeTask(instanceId: string): void {
71	    updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'complete');
72	    ctx.conditionRegistry.clear();
73	    resolveSettle(instanceId);
74	  }
75	
76	  function failTask(instanceId: string, error: string): void {
77	    updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error });
78	    ctx.conditionRegistry.clear();
79	    resolveSettle(instanceId);
80	  }
81	
82	  return { createAbortSignal, abortInstance, resolveSettle, updateLifecycle, completeTask, failTask };
83	}
84	
```

1	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
2	import type {
3	  TaskDefinition,
4	  TaskInstanceState,
5	  TaskStepResult,
6	} from '../core';
7	import { cloneInstanceState, cloneStepResult, isStepResultFailed } from '../core';
8	
9	const MAX_HISTORY = 50;
10	const DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS = 100;
11	
12	// --- Statistics ---
13	
14	export interface TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
15	  readonly totalCreated: number;
16	  readonly totalCompleted: number;
17	  readonly totalStopped: number;
18	  readonly totalFailed: number;
19	  readonly totalStepsExecuted: number;
20	  readonly totalStepsSucceeded: number;
21	  readonly totalStepsFailed: number;
22	  readonly totalStepsSkipped: number;
23	}
24	
25	// --- State snapshot ---
26	
27	export interface TaskStateSnapshot {
28	  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
29	  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
30	  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
31	}
32	
33	export type ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<TaskStateSnapshot>;
34	
35	// --- Container ---
36	
37	export interface TaskStateContainer {
38	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
39	  createInstance(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
40	  updateInstance(
41	    instanceId: string,
42	    updates: Partial<Omit<TaskInstanceState, 'instanceId' | 'definitionRef'>>,
43	  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
44	  removeInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
45	  addStepResult(instanceId: string, result: TaskStepResult): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
46	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
47	  moveToHistory(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
48	  resetStats(): TaskStateSnapshot;
49	}
50	
51	// --- Helpers ---
52	
53	function emptyStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
54	  return {
55	    totalCreated: 0,
56	    totalCompleted: 0,
57	    totalStopped: 0,
58	    totalFailed: 0,
59	    totalStepsExecuted: 0,
60	    totalStepsSucceeded: 0,
61	    totalStepsFailed: 0,
62	    totalStepsSkipped: 0,
63	  };
64	}
65	
66	// --- Factory ---
67	
68	export function createTaskState(
69	  initialValue?: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot },
70	): TaskStateContainer {
71	  const instances = new Map<string, TaskInstanceState>();
72	  let history: TaskInstanceState[] = [];
73	  let statistics = emptyStatistics();
74	
75	  if (initialValue?.snapshot) {
76	    for (const inst of initialValue.snapshot.instances) {
77	      instances.set(inst.instanceId, cloneInstanceState(inst as TaskInstanceState));
78	    }
79	    history = initialValue.snapshot.history.map((inst) => cloneInstanceState(inst as TaskInstanceState));
80	    statistics = { ...initialValue.snapshot.statistics };
81	  }
82	
83	  function getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot {
84	    return {
85	      instances: [...instances.values()].map(cloneInstanceState),
86	      history: history.map(cloneInstanceState),
87	      statistics: { ...statistics },
88	    };
89	  }
90	
91	  return {
92	    getSnapshot,
93	
94	    createInstance(instanceId, definition) {
95	      const instance: TaskInstanceState = {
96	        instanceId,
97	        definitionRef: definition,
98	        lifecycle: 'created',
99	        currentStepIndex: 0,
100	        currentIteration: 0,
101	        stepResults: [],
102	      };
103	      instances.set(instanceId, instance);
104	      statistics = { ...statistics, totalCreated: statistics.totalCreated + 1 };
105	      return cloneInstanceState(instance);
106	    },
107	
108	    updateInstance(instanceId, updates) {
109	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
110	      if (!existing) return undefined;
111	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
112	        ...existing,
113	        ...updates,
114	        instanceId: existing.instanceId,
115	        definitionRef: existing.definitionRef,
116	      };
117	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
118	
119	      if (updates.lifecycle === 'completed') {
120	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalCompleted: statistics.totalCompleted + 1 };
121	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'stopped') {
122	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalStopped: statistics.totalStopped + 1 };
123	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'failed') {
124	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalFailed: statistics.totalFailed + 1 };
125	      }
126	
127	      return cloneInstanceState(updated);
128	    },
129	
130	    removeInstance(instanceId) {
131	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
132	      if (!existing) return undefined;
133	      instances.delete(instanceId);
134	      return cloneInstanceState(existing);
135	    },
136	
137	    addStepResult(instanceId, result) {
138	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
139	      if (!existing) return undefined;
140	
141	      const newResults = [...existing.stepResults, cloneStepResult(result)];
142	      const maxIter = Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration);
143	      const minIteration = Math.max(0, maxIter - DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS + 1);
144	      const boundedResults = newResults.filter((r) => r.iteration >= minIteration);
145	
146	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
147	        ...existing,
148	        stepResults: boundedResults,
149	        currentStepIndex: result.stepIndex,
150	        currentIteration: Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration),
151	      };
152	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
153	
154	      const isSkipped = result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step';
155	      const isFailed = !isSkipped && isStepResultFailed(result);
156	      statistics = {
157	        ...statistics,
158	        totalStepsExecuted: statistics.totalStepsExecuted + 1,
159	        totalStepsSucceeded: statistics.totalStepsSucceeded + (isSkipped || isFailed ? 0 : 1),
160	        totalStepsFailed: statistics.totalStepsFailed + (isFailed ? 1 : 0),
161	        totalStepsSkipped: statistics.totalStepsSkipped + (isSkipped ? 1 : 0),
162	      };
163	
164	      return cloneInstanceState(updated);
165	    },
166	
167	    getInstance(instanceId) {
168	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
169	      return existing ? cloneInstanceState(existing) : undefined;
170	    },
171	
172	    moveToHistory(instanceId) {
173	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
174	      if (!existing) return undefined;
175	      instances.delete(instanceId);
176	      history = [...history, cloneInstanceState(existing)].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);
177	      return cloneInstanceState(existing);
178	    },
179	
180	    resetStats() {
181	      statistics = emptyStatistics();
182	      return getSnapshot();
183	    },
184	  };
185	}
186	
```

11	// --- Helper factories ---

4	export interface FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions {

8	
9	export function createFakeReceiveEventSource(): ReceiveEventSource & {
10	  emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void;
11	  readonly emitted: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
12	} {
13	  const emitted: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
14	  const handlers = new Set<(input: ConditionMatchInput) => void>();
15	
16	  return {
17	    get emitted() {
18	      return emitted;
19	    },
20	
21	    emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
22	      emitted.push(input);
23	      for (const handler of handlers) {
24	        handler(input);
25	      }
26	    },
27	
28	    subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void {
29	      handlers.add(handler);
30	      return () => {
31	        handlers.delete(handler);
32	      };
33	    },
34	  };
35	}
36	
```

1	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type {
3	  TaskInstanceState,
4	  TaskStepResult,
5	  TaskProgress,
6	  TaskExecutionSummary,
7	} from './types';
8	
9	export function cloneStepResult(result: Readonly<TaskStepResult>): TaskStepResult {
10	  const base = {
11	    stepIndex: result.stepIndex,
12	    iteration: result.iteration,
13	    ...(result.appliedPolicy ? { appliedPolicy: result.appliedPolicy } : {}),
14	  };
15	  switch (result.kind) {
16	    case 'send':
17	      return { ...base, kind: 'send', sendResult: cloneSendResultRef(result.sendResult) };
18	    case 'wait-condition':
19	      return {
20	        ...base,
21	        kind: 'wait-condition',
22	        matched: result.matched,
23	        timedOut: result.timedOut,
24	        ...(result.matchedValue !== undefined ? { matchedValue: result.matchedValue } : {}),
25	      };
26	    case 'delay':
27	      return { ...base, kind: 'delay', completed: result.completed };
28	  }
29	}
30	
31	function cloneSendResultRef(result: Readonly<SendResult>): SendResult {
32	  return {
33	    kind: result.kind,
34	    requestRef: {
35	      frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,
36	      targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,
37	      ...(result.requestRef.context ? { context: {
38	        source: result.requestRef.context.source,
39	        ...(result.requestRef.context.taskId !== undefined ? { taskId: result.requestRef.context.taskId } : {}),
40	        ...(result.requestRef.context.stepIndex !== undefined ? { stepIndex: result.requestRef.context.stepIndex } : {}),
41	      } } : {}),
42	    },
43	    bytesBuilt: result.bytesBuilt,
44	    bytesSent: result.bytesSent,
45	    timestamp: result.timestamp,
46	    ...(result.error ? { error: { kind: result.error.kind, message: result.error.message } } : {}),
47	    buildIssues: result.buildIssues.map((i) => ({
48	      severity: i.severity,
49	      code: i.code,
50	      message: i.message,
51	      ...(i.fieldId ? { fieldId: i.fieldId } : {}),
52	    })),
53	  };
54	}
55	
56	export function cloneInstanceState(instance: Readonly<TaskInstanceState>): TaskInstanceState {
57	  return {
58	    instanceId: instance.instanceId,
59	    definitionRef: instance.definitionRef,
60	    lifecycle: instance.lifecycle,
61	    ...(instance.startedAt ? { startedAt: instance.startedAt } : {}),
62	    ...(instance.pausedAt ? { pausedAt: instance.pausedAt } : {}),
63	    ...(instance.stoppedAt ? { stoppedAt: instance.stoppedAt } : {}),
64	    ...(instance.completedAt ? { completedAt: instance.completedAt } : {}),
65	    ...(instance.failedAt ? { failedAt: instance.failedAt } : {}),
66	    currentStepIndex: instance.currentStepIndex,
67	    currentIteration: instance.currentIteration,
68	    stepResults: instance.stepResults.map(cloneStepResult),
69	    ...(instance.error ? { error: instance.error } : {}),
70	  };
71	}
72	
73	export function cloneProgress(progress: Readonly<TaskProgress>): TaskProgress {
74	  return {
75	    stepsTotal: progress.stepsTotal,
76	    stepsCompleted: progress.stepsCompleted,
77	    stepsFailed: progress.stepsFailed,
78	    stepsSkipped: progress.stepsSkipped,
79	    iterationsCompleted: progress.iterationsCompleted,
80	    iterationsTotal: progress.iterationsTotal,
81	    elapsedMs: progress.elapsedMs,
82	    estimatedRemainingMs: progress.estimatedRemainingMs,
83	    lastStepResult: progress.lastStepResult ? cloneStepResult(progress.lastStepResult) : null,
84	  };
85	}
86	
87	export function cloneExecutionSummary(summary: Readonly<TaskExecutionSummary>): TaskExecutionSummary {
88	  return {
89	    kind: summary.kind,
90	    summary: { ...summary.summary },
91	    durationMs: summary.durationMs,
92	    startedAt: summary.startedAt,
93	    finishedAt: summary.finishedAt,
94	  };
95	}
96

1	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepResult } from './types';
2	
3	export function calculateProgress(instance: TaskInstanceState): TaskProgress {
4	  const stepsPerIteration = instance.definitionRef.steps.length;
5	  const { currentIteration, stepResults } = instance;
6	
7	  const currentIterResults = stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIteration);
8	
9	  let stepsCompleted = 0;
10	  let stepsFailed = 0;
11	  let stepsSkipped = 0;
12	
13	  for (const result of currentIterResults) {
14	    if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {
15	      stepsSkipped++;
16	    } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {
17	      stepsFailed++;
18	    } else {
19	      stepsCompleted++;
20	    }
21	  }
22	
23	  const iterationsCompleted = countCompletedIterations(instance, stepsPerIteration);
24	  const iterationsTotal = resolveIterationsTotal(instance);
25	  const elapsedMs = calculateElapsedMs(instance);
26	
27	  const estimatedRemainingMs = estimateRemainingMs(instance, iterationsCompleted, iterationsTotal, elapsedMs);
28	  const lastStepResult = stepResults.length > 0 ? stepResults[stepResults.length - 1]! : null;
29	
30	  return {
31	    stepsTotal: stepsPerIteration,
32	    stepsCompleted,
33	    stepsFailed,
34	    stepsSkipped,
35	    iterationsCompleted,
36	    iterationsTotal,
37	    elapsedMs,
38	    estimatedRemainingMs,
39	    lastStepResult,
40	  };
41	}
42	
43	export function isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {
44	  switch (result.kind) {
45	    case 'send':
46	      return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
47	    case 'wait-condition':
48	      return !result.matched && result.timedOut;
49	    case 'delay':
50	      return !result.completed;
51	  }
52	}
53	
54	function countCompletedIterations(instance: TaskInstanceState, stepsPerIteration: number): number {
55	  const iterationIndices = new Set(instance.stepResults.map((r) => r.iteration));
56	  let count = 0;
57	  for (const iter of iterationIndices) {
58	    const iterResults = instance.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === iter);
59	    if (iterResults.length >= stepsPerIteration) {
60	      count++;
61	    }
62	  }
63	  return count;
64	}
65	
66	function resolveIterationsTotal(instance: TaskInstanceState): number | null {
67	  const { schedule, stopCondition } = instance.definitionRef;
68	  if (schedule.kind === 'timer') {
69	    return stopCondition?.maxIterations ?? null;
70	  }
71	  if (schedule.kind === 'immediate') {
72	    return 1;
73	  }
74	  return null;
75	}
76	
77	function calculateElapsedMs(instance: TaskInstanceState): number {
78	  if (!instance.startedAt) return 0;
79	  const start = new Date(instance.startedAt).getTime();
80	  const endRef = instance.completedAt ?? instance.stoppedAt ?? instance.failedAt;
81	  const end = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
82	  return Math.max(0, end - start);
83	}
84	
85	function estimateRemainingMs(
86	  instance: TaskInstanceState,
87	  iterationsCompleted: number,
88	  iterationsTotal: number | null,
89	  elapsedMs: number,
90	): number | null {
91	  if (instance.definitionRef.schedule.kind !== 'timer' || iterationsTotal === null) {
92	    return null;
93	  }
94	  if (iterationsCompleted <= 0 || iterationsTotal <= iterationsCompleted) {
95	    return null;
96	  }
97	  const avgIterMs = elapsedMs / iterationsCompleted;
98	  return Math.round(avgIterMs * (iterationsTotal - iterationsCompleted));
99	}
100	
```

1	import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
2	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { TaskDefinition } from '../core';
4	import { timedTaskDef, triggerTaskDef, sequenceTaskDef, scoeModeTaskDef, errorPolicies, makeSendResult } from '../fixtures/task-fixtures';
5	import { createFakeSendService } from '../adapters/test-exports';

11	// --- Helpers ---
12	
13	function makeSentResult(): SendResult {
14	  return makeSendResult();
15	}
16	
17	function makeErrorResult(kind: 'transport-error' | 'timeout' | 'target-unavailable' | 'build-error' = 'transport-error'): SendResult {
18	  return makeSendResult({ kind }) as SendResult;
19	}
20	
21	function createTestSetup(overrides: { definition?: TaskDefinition; sendResults?: readonly SendResult[] } = {}) {
22	  const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results: overrides.sendResults ?? [makeSentResult()] });
23	  const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();
24	  const service = createTaskService({
25	    sendService: fakeSend,
26	    receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,
27	    now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',
28	  });
29	  const definition = overrides.definition ?? timedTaskDef();
30	  const instance = service.createTask(definition);
31	  return { service, fakeSend, fakeReceive, definition, instance };
32	}
33	
34	async function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<void> {
35	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
36	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
37	    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);
38	    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
39	      return;
40	    }
41	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
42	  }
43	}
44

49	describe('TaskService - timed scheduling', () => {
50	  it('executes N iterations then completes', async () => {
51	    const maxCount = 3;
52	    const def: TaskDefinition = { ...timedTaskDef(), stopCondition: { maxIterations: maxCount }, schedule: { kind: 'timer', intervalMs: 10 } };
53	    const results = Array.from({ length: maxCount * def.steps.length }, () => makeSentResult());
54	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
55	
56	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
57	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId);
58	
59	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
60	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
61	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(maxCount * def.steps.length);
62	  });
63	
64	  it('tracks iterations in step results', async () => {

67	    const results = Array.from({ length: maxCount * def.steps.length }, () => makeSentResult());
68	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });

483	    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
484	      const check = () => {

486	        if (inst && inst.stepResults.length >= 1 && inst.lifecycle === 'running') {
487	          resolve();
488	        } else {
489	          setTimeout(check, 5);
490	        }
491	      };
492	      setTimeout(check, 5);
493	    });
494	
495	    service.pauseTask(instance.instanceId);
496	
497	    const paused = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
498	    expect(paused?.lifecycle).toBe('paused');
499	    // Should have at least 1 step result
500	    expect(paused!.stepResults.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
501	
502	    service.stopTask(instance.instanceId);
503	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 100);
504	  });
505	
506	  it('resume continues execution from paused point', async () => {
507	    // Sequence: send(1) → delay(500ms) → send(2)
508	    // Plan: start, let first send complete, pause during delay, then resume
509	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
510	      id: 'pause-resume-test',
511	      name: 'Pause Resume Test',
512	      schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
513	      steps: [
514	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', config: { frameId: 'f1', targetId: 'target-1', userFieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
515	        { id: 's2', kind: 'delay', config: { durationMs: 500 } },
516	        { id: 's3', kind: 'send', config: { frameId: 'f2', targetId: 'target-1', userFieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
517	      ],
518	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
519	    };

521	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
522	
523	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
524	    // Wait for first send step to complete (delay step hasn't finished yet)
525	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
526	
527	    const midExec = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
528	    // Pause during the delay step

530	      service.pauseTask(instance.instanceId);
531	    }
532	
533	    const paused = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
534	    expect(paused?.lifecycle).toBe('paused');
535	    // Should have 1 step result (first send completed)
536	    expect(paused!.stepResults.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
537	
538	    // Now resume — small delay ensures the old execution loop has fully settled
539	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
540	    service.resumeTask(instance.instanceId);
541	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 3000);
542	
543	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
544	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
545	    // Both sends should have been executed (1 before pause + 1 after resume)
546	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2);
547	    // All 3 steps should have results
548	    expect(final!.stepResults.length).toBe(3);
549	  });

1	import type { TaskLifecycleStatus, LifecycleAction } from './types';
2	
3	const VALID_TRANSITIONS: Record<TaskLifecycleStatus, readonly TaskLifecycleStatus[]> = {
4	  created: ['running'],
5	  running: ['paused', 'stopped', 'completed', 'failed'],
6	  paused: ['running', 'stopped'],
7	  stopped: [],
8	  completed: [],
9	  failed: [],
10	};
11	
12	const ACTION_TARGETS: Record<LifecycleAction, readonly { from: TaskLifecycleStatus; to: TaskLifecycleStatus }[]> = {
13	  start: [{ from: 'created', to: 'running' }],
14	  pause: [{ from: 'running', to: 'paused' }],
15	  resume: [{ from: 'paused', to: 'running' }],
16	  stop: [{ from: 'running', to: 'stopped' }, { from: 'paused', to: 'stopped' }],
17	  complete: [{ from: 'running', to: 'completed' }],
18	  fail: [{ from: 'running', to: 'failed' }],
19	};
20	
21	export function canTransition(from: TaskLifecycleStatus, to: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean {
22	  return VALID_TRANSITIONS[from].includes(to);
23	}
24	
25	export function transition(state: TaskLifecycleStatus, action: LifecycleAction): TaskLifecycleStatus {
26	  const targets = ACTION_TARGETS[action];
27	  const match = targets.find((t) => t.from === state);
28	  if (!match) {
29	    throw new Error(`Invalid lifecycle transition: cannot '${action}' from '${state}'`);
30	  }
31	  return match.to;
32	}
33	
34	export function isTerminal(status: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean {
35	  return status === 'stopped' || status === 'completed' || status === 'failed';
36	}
37	
```

  "old_string": "import type { SendRequest } from '@/features/send';\nimport type { TaskDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '../core';\nimport type { SendServiceProvider } from '../adapters';\nimport type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';\nimport type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';\n\n// --- Standalone helpers ---\n\nexport function sleep(ms: number): { promise: Promise<void>; cancel: () => void } {\n  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;\n  const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n    timer = setTimeout(resolve, ms);\n  });\n  return { promise, cancel: () => clearTimeout(timer) };\n}\n\nexport function buildSendRequest(\n  stepConfig: TaskDefinition['steps'][number] & { kind: 'send' },\n  definition: TaskDefinition,\n  stepIndex: number,\n): SendRequest {\n  return {\n    frameId: stepConfig.config.frameId,\n    targetId: stepConfig.config.targetId,\n    ...(stepConfig.config.userFieldValues ? { userFieldValues: stepConfig.config.userFieldValues } : {}),\n    context: { source: 'task' as const, taskId: definition.id, stepIndex },\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "import type { SendRequest } from '@/features/send';\nimport type { TaskDefinition, TaskStepResult, FieldVariation } from '../core';\nimport type { SendServiceProvider } from '../adapters';\nimport type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';\nimport type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';\n\n// --- Standalone helpers ---\n\nexport function sleep(ms: number): { promise: Promise<void>; cancel: () => void } {\n  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;\n  const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n    timer = setTimeout(resolve, ms);\n  });\n  return { promise, cancel: () => clearTimeout(timer) };\n}\n\nexport function resolveFieldValues(\n  baseValues: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> | undefined,\n  fieldVariations: readonly FieldVariation[] | undefined,\n  iteration: number,\n): Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> {\n  if (!fieldVariations || fieldVariations.length === 0) return baseValues ?? {};\n  const result: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = { ...(baseValues ?? {}) };\n  for (const variation of fieldVariations) {\n    if (iteration < variation.values.length) {\n      result[variation.fieldId] = variation.values[iteration];\n    }\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n\nexport function buildSendRequest(\n  stepConfig: TaskDefinition['steps'][number] & { kind: 'send' },\n  definition: TaskDefinition,\n  stepIndex: number,\n  iteration: number,\n): SendRequest {\n  return {\n    frameId: stepConfig.config.frameId,\n    targetId: stepConfig.config.targetId,\n    userFieldValues: resolveFieldValues(\n      stepConfig.config.userFieldValues,\n      definition.fieldVariations,\n      iteration,\n    ),\n    ...(stepConfig.config.variables ? { variables: stepConfig.config.variables } : {}),\n    context: { source: 'task' as const, taskId: definition.id, stepIndex },\n  };\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/services/task-iteration-loops.ts",
  "content": "import type {\n  TaskDefinition,\n  TaskStepResult,\n  ConditionTerm,\n  ResolvedStopCondition,\n  ScheduleDriver,\n} from '../core';\nimport { evaluateConditionGroup } from '../core';\nimport type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';\nimport type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';\nimport type { UpdateLifecycleFn } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';\nimport type { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';\nimport { sleep } from './task-step-executors';\nimport type { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';\n\n// --- Iteration loop context ---\n\nexport interface IterationLoopContext {\n  state: TaskStateContainer;\n  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;\n  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;\n  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;\n  errorPolicy: ReturnType<typeof createErrorPolicyHandler>;\n  fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;\n  now: () => string;\n}\n\n// --- ScheduleDriver adapter ---\n\ninterface ScheduleDriverAdapter {\n  waitForNext(instanceId: string, iteration: number, signal: Promise<void>): Promise<boolean>;\n  dispose(): void;\n}\n\nfunction createImmediateDriver(): ScheduleDriverAdapter {\n  return { waitForNext: async () => true, dispose: () => {} };\n}\n\nfunction createTimerDriver(intervalMs: number): ScheduleDriverAdapter {\n  let firstCall = true;\n\n  return {\n    async waitForNext(_instanceId, _iteration, signal) {\n      if (firstCall) { firstCall = false; return true; }\n\n      const delay = sleep(intervalMs);\n      const race = await Promise.race([\n        delay.promise.then(() => true),\n        signal.then(() => false),\n      ]);\n      if (!race) delay.cancel();\n      return race;\n    },\n    dispose() {},\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createEventDriver(\n  schedule: Extract<ScheduleDriver, { kind: 'event' }>,\n  ctx: IterationLoopContext,\n  signal: Promise<void>,\n): ScheduleDriverAdapter {\n  const { conditions, cooldownMs = 0 } = schedule;\n  let lastTriggerTime = 0;\n  let notifyResolve: ((value: boolean) => void) | null = null;\n  let disposed = false;\n\n  const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {\n    if (disposed) return;\n    const now = Date.now();\n    if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && now - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;\n    lastTriggerTime = now;\n    if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(true); notifyResolve = null; }\n  });\n\n  return {\n    async waitForNext() {\n      if (disposed) return false;\n      return Promise.race([\n        new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => { notifyResolve = resolve; }),\n        signal.then(() => false),\n      ]);\n    },\n    dispose() {\n      disposed = true;\n      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);\n      if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(false); notifyResolve = null; }\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createDriver(\n  schedule: ScheduleDriver,\n  ctx: IterationLoopContext,\n  signal: Promise<void>,\n): ScheduleDriverAdapter {\n  switch (schedule.kind) {\n    case 'immediate':\n      return createImmediateDriver();\n    case 'timer':\n      return createTimerDriver(schedule.intervalMs);\n    case 'event':\n      return createEventDriver(schedule, ctx, signal);\n  }\n}\n\n// --- StopGuard ---\n\ninterface StopGuard {\n  shouldStop(instanceId: string, iteration: number): boolean;\n  checkExitCondition(): boolean;\n}\n\nfunction createStopGuard(stop: ResolvedStopCondition, ctx: IterationLoopContext): StopGuard {\n  const startTime = Date.now();\n\n  return {\n    shouldStop(instanceId, iteration) {\n      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return true;\n      if (stop.maxIterations !== undefined && iteration >= stop.maxIterations) return true;\n      if (stop.maxDurationMs !== undefined && Date.now() - startTime >= stop.maxDurationMs) return true;\n      return false;\n    },\n    checkExitCondition() {\n      if (!stop.exitCondition) return false;\n      if (!ctx.fieldValueProvider) return false;\n      return evaluateConditionGroup(stop.exitCondition, ctx.fieldValueProvider());\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// --- Main loop factory ---\n\nexport function createIterationLoops(ctx: IterationLoopContext) {\n  // --- executeStepCore ---\n\n  async function executeStepCore(\n    instanceId: string,\n    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {\n    switch (step.kind) {\n      case 'send': {\n        if (step.config.repeat) {\n          return executeRepeatableSend(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);\n        }\n        const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);\n        if (result.kind === 'send' && result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {\n          return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);\n        }\n        return { result, shouldStop: false };\n      }\n\n      case 'wait-condition': {\n        const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(\n          instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,\n        );\n        if (interrupted) return null;\n\n        if (result.kind === 'wait-condition' && !result.matched && result.timedOut) {\n          switch (step.config.onTimeout) {\n            case 'continue':\n              return { result, shouldStop: false };\n            case 'skip':\n              return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' } as TaskStepResult, shouldStop: false };\n            case 'fail':\n              return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);\n          }\n        }\n        return { result, shouldStop: false };\n      }\n\n      case 'delay': {\n        const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(\n          iteration, stepIndex, step.config.durationMs, signal,\n        );\n        if (interrupted) return null;\n        return { result, shouldStop: false };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // --- executeRepeatableSend ---\n\n  async function executeRepeatableSend(\n    instanceId: string,\n    step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'send' }>,\n    iteration: number,\n    stepIndex: number,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {\n    const repeat = step.config.repeat;\n    let count = 0;\n    const maxCount = repeat!.maxCount ?? Infinity;\n    let lastResult: TaskStepResult | null = null;\n\n    while (count < maxCount) {\n      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return null;\n\n      const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);\n      ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);\n\n      if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {\n        const errOutcome = await ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(\n          instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal,\n        );\n        if (errOutcome) ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, errOutcome.result);\n        return errOutcome;\n      }\n\n      lastResult = result;\n      count++;\n\n      if (repeat!.until && ctx.fieldValueProvider) {\n        if (evaluateConditionGroup(repeat!.until, ctx.fieldValueProvider())) break;\n      }\n\n      if (count < maxCount) {\n        const delay = sleep(repeat!.intervalMs);\n        const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => true), signal.then(() => false)]);\n        if (!race) { delay.cancel(); return null; }\n      }\n    }\n\n    return lastResult ? { result: lastResult, shouldStop: false } : null;\n  }\n\n  // --- executeSteps ---\n\n  async function executeSteps(\n    instanceId: string,\n    iteration: number,\n    steps: TaskDefinition['steps'],\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<boolean> {\n    const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);\n    if (!inst) return false;\n    const completedInIter = inst.stepResults\n      .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)\n      .map((r) => r.stepIndex);\n    const startIndex = completedInIter.length;\n\n    for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {\n      const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;\n\n      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });\n\n      const step = steps[i];\n      if (!step) continue;\n\n      const outcome = await executeStepCore(\n        instanceId, step, iteration, i, definition, signal,\n      );\n\n      if (outcome === null) return false;\n\n      // For repeat sends, results are already added inside executeRepeatableSend\n      const isRepeatSend = step.kind === 'send' && !!step.config.repeat;\n      if (!isRepeatSend) {\n        ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);\n      }\n\n      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;\n\n      // intervalAfterMs after send steps\n      if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {\n        const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);\n        const race = await Promise.race([\n          delay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),\n          signal.then(() => 'intr' as const),\n        ]);\n        if (race === 'intr') { delay.cancel(); return false; }\n      }\n    }\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  // --- Unified runTask ---\n\n  async function runTask(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const driver = createDriver(definition.schedule, ctx, signal);\n    const stopGuard = createStopGuard(resolvedStop, ctx);\n\n    let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;\n\n    while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {\n      const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);\n      if (!triggered) break;\n\n      const completed = await executeSteps(\n        instanceId, iteration, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n      );\n      if (!completed) break;\n\n      if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;\n\n      iteration++;\n      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });\n    }\n\n    driver.dispose();\n  }\n\n  return { runTask };\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "  async function runTask(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const driver = createDriver(definition.schedule, ctx, signal);\n    const stopGuard = createStopGuard(resolvedStop, ctx);\n\n    let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;\n\n    while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {\n      const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);\n      if (!triggered) break;\n\n      const completed = await executeSteps(\n        instanceId, iteration, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n      );\n      if (!completed) break;\n\n      if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;\n\n      iteration++;\n      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });\n    }\n\n    driver.dispose();\n  }",
  "new_string": "  async function runTask(\n    instanceId: string,\n    definition: TaskDefinition,\n    resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition,\n    signal: Promise<void>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const driver = createDriver(definition.schedule, ctx, signal);\n\n    const effectiveStop: ResolvedStopCondition =\n      definition.schedule.kind === 'immediate' && resolvedStop.maxIterations === undefined\n        ? { ...resolvedStop, maxIterations: 1 }\n        : resolvedStop;\n    const stopGuard = createStopGuard(effectiveStop, ctx);\n\n    let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;\n\n    while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {\n      const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);\n      if (!triggered) break;\n\n      const completed = await executeSteps(\n        instanceId, iteration, definition.steps, definition, signal,\n      );\n      if (!completed) break;\n\n      if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;\n\n      iteration++;\n      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });\n    }\n\n    driver.dispose();\n  }"
}

> AGENT

问题是循环结束后 `currentIteration` 被设为 1，但 stepResult 的 iteration 是 0，导致 progress 计算时过滤不到结果。修复：不手动更新 `currentIteration`，让 `addStepResult` 自动管理。

========================================================================\n// ExitCondition\n// ========================================================================\n\ndescribe('TaskService - exitCondition', () => {\n  it('stops task when exitCondition is met', async () => {\n    let callCount = 0;\n    const fieldValueProvider = () => {\n      callCount++;\n      return callCount >= 3 ? { done: 1 } : { done: 0 };\n    };\n\n    const def: TaskDefinition = {\n      id: 'exit-cond',\n      name: 'Exit Condition Test',\n      schedule: { kind: 'timer', intervalMs: 10 },\n      steps: [{\n        id: 's1', kind: 'send',\n        config: { frameId: 'f1', targetId: 't1' },\n      }],\n      stopCondition: {\n        maxIterations: 100,\n        exitCondition: [{ frameId: 'f1', fieldId: 'done', operator: 'eq', threshold: 1 }],\n      },\n      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },\n    };\n    const results = Array.from({ length: 100 }, () => makeSentResult());\n    const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results });\n    const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();\n    const service = createTaskService({\n      sendService: fakeSend,\n      receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,\n      fieldValueProvider,\n      now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',\n    });\n    const inst = service.createTask(def);\n\n    service.startTask(inst.instanceId);\n    await settle(service, inst.instanceId, 3000);\n\n    const final = service.getInstance(inst.instanceId);\n    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');\n    // Should have stopped early due to exitCondition, not after 100 iterations\n    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBeLessThan(10);\n    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);\n  });\n});\n\n// ========================================================================\n// State / Selectors\n// ========================================================================"
}

## 实施完成

### Changed files

| 文件 | 变更 |
|------|------|
| `services/task-step-executors.ts` | 新增 `resolveFieldValues`；`buildSendRequest` 增加 `iteration` 参数，集成 fieldVariations 注入和 variables 透传 |
| `services/task-iteration-loops.ts` | **Major rewrite** — 移除 `runTimedLoop`/`runTriggerLoop`/`runSequenceLoop`，新增统一 `runTask` + `createDriver`(immediate/timer/event) + `createStopGuard`(maxIterations + maxDurationMs + exitCondition) + `executeRepeatableSend`；context 新增 `fieldValueProvider`；`executeSteps` 增加 `intervalAfterMs` 和 repeat 结果去重 |
| `services/task-service.ts` | `runExecutionLoop` 从 `switch(schedule.kind)` 改为 `runTask` 调用 + `resolveStopCondition`；`CreateTaskServiceOptions` 新增 `timerService?` / `fieldValueProvider?` |
| `adapters/ports.ts` | 新增 `TimerService` 接口 |
| `adapters/index.ts` | 导出 `TimerService` |
| `index.ts` | 导出 `TimerService` |
| `__tests__/task-core.spec.ts` | 新增 evaluateConditionGroup 测试（AND/OR/mixed/短路/空/单条件，20 项）+ ConditionRegistry 组注册测试（AND/OR组/unregister/frameId索引/sourceId过滤/clear，8 项） |
| `__tests__/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts` | 新增 resolveFieldValues（5 项）、repeat send（maxCount + until，2 项）、fieldVariations 自动 maxIterations 注入验证（1 项）、exitCondition（1 项） |

1	import type {
2	  TaskDefinition,
3	  TaskStepResult,
4	  ResolvedStopCondition,
5	  ScheduleDriver,
6	} from '../core';
7	import { evaluateConditionGroup } from '../core';
8	import type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
9	import type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
10	import type { UpdateLifecycleFn } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
11	import type { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
12	import { sleep } from './task-step-executors';
13	import type { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';
14	
15	// --- Iteration loop context ---
16	
17	export interface IterationLoopContext {
18	  state: TaskStateContainer;
19	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
20	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
21	  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;
22	  errorPolicy: ReturnType<typeof createErrorPolicyHandler>;
23	  fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
24	  now: () => string;
25	}
26	
27	// --- ScheduleDriver adapter ---
28	
29	interface ScheduleDriverAdapter {
30	  waitForNext(instanceId: string, iteration: number, signal: Promise<void>): Promise<boolean>;
31	  dispose(): void;
32	}
33	
34	function createImmediateDriver(): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
35	  return { waitForNext: async () => true, dispose: () => {} };
36	}
37	
38	function createTimerDriver(intervalMs: number): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
39	  let firstCall = true;
40	
41	  return {
42	    async waitForNext(_instanceId, _iteration, signal) {
43	      if (firstCall) { firstCall = false; return true; }
44	
45	      const delay = sleep(intervalMs);
46	      const race = await Promise.race([
47	        delay.promise.then(() => true),
48	        signal.then(() => false),
49	      ]);
50	      if (!race) delay.cancel();
51	      return race;
52	    },
53	    dispose() {},
54	  };
55	}
56	
57	function createEventDriver(
58	  schedule: Extract<ScheduleDriver, { kind: 'event' }>,
59	  ctx: IterationLoopContext,
60	  signal: Promise<void>,
61	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
62	  const { conditions, cooldownMs = 0 } = schedule;
63	  let lastTriggerTime = 0;
64	  let notifyResolve: ((value: boolean) => void) | null = null;
65	  let disposed = false;
66	
67	  const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
68	    if (disposed) return;
69	    const now = Date.now();
70	    if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && now - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
71	    lastTriggerTime = now;
72	    if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(true); notifyResolve = null; }
73	  });
74	
75	  return {
76	    async waitForNext() {
77	      if (disposed) return false;
78	      return Promise.race([
79	        new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => { notifyResolve = resolve; }),
80	        signal.then(() => false),
81	      ]);
82	    },
83	    dispose() {
84	      disposed = true;
85	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
86	      if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(false); notifyResolve = null; }
87	    },
88	  };
89	}
90	
91	function createDriver(
92	  schedule: ScheduleDriver,
93	  ctx: IterationLoopContext,
94	  signal: Promise<void>,
95	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
96	  switch (schedule.kind) {
97	    case 'immediate':
98	      return createImmediateDriver();
99	    case 'timer':
100	      return createTimerDriver(schedule.intervalMs);
101	    case 'event':
102	      return createEventDriver(schedule, ctx, signal);
103	  }
104	}
105	
106	// --- StopGuard ---
107	
108	interface StopGuard {
109	  shouldStop(instanceId: string, iteration: number): boolean;
110	  checkExitCondition(): boolean;
111	}
112	
113	function createStopGuard(stop: ResolvedStopCondition, ctx: IterationLoopContext): StopGuard {
114	  const startTime = Date.now();
115	
116	  return {
117	    shouldStop(instanceId, iteration) {
118	      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
119	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return true;
120	      if (stop.maxIterations !== undefined && iteration >= stop.maxIterations) return true;
121	      if (stop.maxDurationMs !== undefined && Date.now() - startTime >= stop.maxDurationMs) return true;
122	      return false;
123	    },
124	    checkExitCondition() {
125	      if (!stop.exitCondition) return false;
126	      if (!ctx.fieldValueProvider) return false;
127	      return evaluateConditionGroup(stop.exitCondition, ctx.fieldValueProvider());
128	    },
129	  };
130	}
131	
132	// --- Main loop factory ---
133	
134	export function createIterationLoops(ctx: IterationLoopContext) {
135	  // --- executeStepCore ---
136	
137	  async function executeStepCore(
138	    instanceId: string,
139	    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
140	    iteration: number,
141	    stepIndex: number,
142	    definition: TaskDefinition,
143	    signal: Promise<void>,
144	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
145	    switch (step.kind) {
146	      case 'send': {
147	        if (step.config.repeat) {
148	          return executeRepeatableSend(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
149	        }
150	        const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
151	        if (result.kind === 'send' && result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
152	          return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
153	        }
154	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
155	      }
156	
157	      case 'wait-condition': {
158	        const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(
159	          instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,
160	        );
161	        if (interrupted) return null;
162	
163	        if (result.kind === 'wait-condition' && !result.matched && result.timedOut) {
164	          switch (step.config.onTimeout) {
165	            case 'continue':
166	              return { result, shouldStop: false };
167	            case 'skip':
168	              return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' } as TaskStepResult, shouldStop: false };
169	            case 'fail':
170	              return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
171	          }
172	        }
173	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
174	      }
175	
176	      case 'delay': {
177	        const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(
178	          iteration, stepIndex, step.config.durationMs, signal,
179	        );
180	        if (interrupted) return null;
181	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
182	      }
183	    }
184	  }
185	
186	  // --- executeRepeatableSend ---
187	
188	  async function executeRepeatableSend(
189	    instanceId: string,
190	    step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'send' }>,
191	    iteration: number,
192	    stepIndex: number,
193	    definition: TaskDefinition,
194	    signal: Promise<void>,
195	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
196	    const repeat = step.config.repeat;
197	    let count = 0;
198	    const maxCount = repeat!.maxCount ?? Infinity;
199	    let lastResult: TaskStepResult | null = null;
200	
201	    while (count < maxCount) {
202	      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
203	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return null;
204	
205	      const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
206	      ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
207	
208	      if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
209	        const errOutcome = await ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(
210	          instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal,
211	        );
212	        if (errOutcome) ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, errOutcome.result);
213	        return errOutcome;
214	      }
215	
216	      lastResult = result;
217	      count++;
218	
219	      if (repeat!.until && ctx.fieldValueProvider) {
220	        if (evaluateConditionGroup(repeat!.until, ctx.fieldValueProvider())) break;
221	      }
222	
223	      if (count < maxCount) {
224	        const delay = sleep(repeat!.intervalMs);
225	        const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => true), signal.then(() => false)]);
226	        if (!race) { delay.cancel(); return null; }
227	      }
228	    }
229	
230	    return lastResult ? { result: lastResult, shouldStop: false } : null;
231	  }
232	
233	  // --- executeSteps ---
234	
235	  async function executeSteps(
236	    instanceId: string,
237	    iteration: number,
238	    steps: TaskDefinition['steps'],
239	    definition: TaskDefinition,
240	    signal: Promise<void>,
241	  ): Promise<boolean> {
242	    const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
243	    if (!inst) return false;
244	    const completedInIter = inst.stepResults
245	      .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)
246	      .map((r) => r.stepIndex);
247	    const startIndex = completedInIter.length;
248	
249	    for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {
250	      const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
251	      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;
252	
253	      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });
254	
255	      const step = steps[i];
256	      if (!step) continue;
257	
258	      const outcome = await executeStepCore(
259	        instanceId, step, iteration, i, definition, signal,
260	      );
261	
262	      if (outcome === null) return false;
263	
264	      // For repeat sends, results are already added inside executeRepeatableSend
265	      const isRepeatSend = step.kind === 'send' && !!step.config.repeat;
266	      if (!isRepeatSend) {
267	        ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);
268	      }
269	
270	      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;
271	
272	      // intervalAfterMs after send steps
273	      if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {
274	        const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);
275	        const race = await Promise.race([
276	          delay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),
277	          signal.then(() => 'intr' as const),
278	        ]);
279	        if (race === 'intr') { delay.cancel(); return false; }
280	      }
281	    }
282	    return true;
283	  }
284	
285	  // --- Unified runTask ---
286	
287	  async function runTask(
288	    instanceId: string,
289	    definition: TaskDefinition,
290	    resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition,
291	    signal: Promise<void>,
292	  ): Promise<void> {
293	    const driver = createDriver(definition.schedule, ctx, signal);
294	
295	    const effectiveStop: ResolvedStopCondition =
296	      definition.schedule.kind === 'immediate' && resolvedStop.maxIterations === undefined
297	        ? { ...resolvedStop, maxIterations: 1 }
298	        : resolvedStop;
299	    const stopGuard = createStopGuard(effectiveStop, ctx);
300	
301	    let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;
302	
303	    while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
304	      const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);
305	      if (!triggered) break;
306	
307	      const completed = await executeSteps(
308	        instanceId, iteration, definition.steps, definition, signal,
309	      );
310	      if (!completed) break;
311	
312	      if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;
313	
314	      iteration++;
315	    }
316	
317	    driver.dispose();
318	  }
319	
320	  return { runTask };
321	}
322	
```

3. services/task-service.ts — runExecutionLoop 从 switch 改为 runTask 调用；新增 fieldValueProvider/timerService 选项

审查要求：请从以下所有维度逐项检查，每个维度给出 pass / pass-with-known-gaps / revise-required / blocked：

维度 1：直接合同对齐

维度 2：旧行为等价性（checklist c7）

维度 3：架构约束（checklist c1-c4, c13, c14, c15, c16, c18）

维度 5：新增能力正确性（checklist c5, c6, c9, c10, c11, c12）

维度 7：过渡设计合理性（over-design review）

- effectiveStop 的 immediate 兜底逻辑放在 runTask 内部 vs 放在 resolveStopCondition 中的取舍

每个维度给出结论和具体发现。最后给出整体结论：pass / pass-with-known-gaps / revise-required / blocked。
